            <div class="main-content lit-style" itemscope="itemscope" itemtype="http://schema.org/CreativeWork"><div class="meta-content fm-sec"><h1 id="_NBK320989_"><span class="title" itemprop="name">Nuclear Gene-Encoded Leigh Syndrome Spectrum Overview</span></h1><p class="contrib-group"><span itemprop="author">Shamima Rahman</span>, FRCP, FRCPCH, PhD, <span itemprop="author">David R Thorburn</span>, PhD, and <span itemprop="author">Megan Ball</span>, MD.</p><a data-jig="ncbitoggler" href="#__NBK320989_ai__" style="border:0;text-decoration:none">Author Information and Affiliations</a><div style="display:none" class="ui-widget" id="__NBK320989_ai__"><div class="contrib half_rhythm"><span itemprop="author">Shamima Rahman</span>, FRCP, FRCPCH, PhD<div class="affiliation small">Professor of Paediatric Metabolic Medicine<br />Genetics and Genomic Medicine Programme<br />UCL Institute of Child Health and Great Ormond Street Hospital<br />London, United Kingdom<div><span class="email-label">Email: </span><a href="mailto:dev@null" data-email="ku.ca.lcu@namhar.amimahs" class="oemail">ku.ca.lcu@namhar.amimahs</a></div></div></div><div class="contrib half_rhythm"><span itemprop="author">David R Thorburn</span>, PhD<div class="affiliation small">Senior Principal Research Fellow, Murdoch Children's Research Institute and Genetic Health Services Victoria<br />Victoria, Australia</div><div class="affiliation small">Royal Children's Hospital<br />Victoria, Australia</div><div class="affiliation small">Department of Paediatrics<br />University of Melbourne<br />Melbourne, Australia<div><span class="email-label">Email: </span><a href="mailto:dev@null" data-email="ua.ude.ircm@nrubroht.divad" class="oemail">ua.ude.ircm@nrubroht.divad</a></div></div></div><div class="contrib half_rhythm"><span itemprop="author">Megan Ball</span>, MD<div class="affiliation small">Royal Children's Hospital<br />Victoria, Australia</div><div class="affiliation small">Department of Paediatrics<br />University of Melbourne<br />Melbourne, Australia</div><div class="affiliation small">Metabolic Fellow, PhD Candidate, Murdoch Children's Research Institute<br />Victoria, Australia<div><span class="email-label">Email: </span><a href="mailto:dev@null" data-email="ua.ude.ircm@llab.nagem" class="oemail">ua.ude.ircm@llab.nagem</a></div></div></div></div><p class="small">Initial Posting: <span itemprop="datePublished">October 1, 2015</span>; Last Update: <span itemprop="dateModified">May 1, 2025</span>.</p><p><em>Estimated reading time: 1 hour</em></p></div><div class="jig-ncbiinpagenav body-content whole_rhythm" data-jigconfig="allHeadingLevels: ['h2'],smoothScroll: false" itemprop="text"><div id="leigh-nucl-ov.Summary" itemprop="description"><h2 id="_leigh-nucl-ov_Summary_">Summary</h2><p>The purpose of this overview is to:</p><dl class="temp-labeled-list"><dt>1.</dt><dd><p class="no_top_margin">Briefly describe the <a href="#leigh-nucl-ov.Clinical_Characteristics_o">clinical characteristics</a> of nuclear <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/gene/">gene</a>-encoded Leigh syndrome spectrum (LSS);</p></dd><dt>2.</dt><dd><p class="no_top_margin">Review the <a href="#leigh-nucl-ov.Causes_of_Nuclear_GeneEnco">genetic causes</a> of nuclear <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/gene/">gene</a>-encoded LSS;</p></dd><dt>3.</dt><dd><p class="no_top_margin">Review the <a href="#leigh-nucl-ov.Differential_Diagnosis_of">differential diagnosis</a> of nuclear <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/gene/">gene</a>-encoded LSS with a focus on genetic conditions;</p></dd><dt>4.</dt><dd><p class="no_top_margin">Provide an <a href="#leigh-nucl-ov.Evaluation_Strategies_to_I">evaluation strategy</a> to identify the genetic cause of nuclear <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/gene/">gene</a>-encoded LSS in a <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/proband/">proband</a> (when possible);</p></dd><dt>5.</dt><dd><p class="no_top_margin">Review <a href="#leigh-nucl-ov.Management_of_Nuclear_Gene">management</a> of nuclear <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/gene/">gene</a>-encoded LSS with a focus on disorders with targeted therapies;</p></dd><dt>6.</dt><dd><p class="no_top_margin">Inform <a href="#leigh-nucl-ov.Genetic_Counseling">genetic counseling</a> of family members of an individual with nuclear <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/gene/">gene</a>-encoded LSS.</p></dd></dl>
</div><div id="leigh-nucl-ov.Clinical_Characteristics_o"><h2 id="_leigh-nucl-ov_Clinical_Characteristics_o_">1.  Clinical Characteristics of Nuclear Gene-Encoded Leigh Syndrome Spectrum</h2><p>Nuclear <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/gene/">gene</a>-encoded Leigh syndrome spectrum (LSS) is a continuum of progressive neurodegenerative disorders caused by abnormalities of mitochondrial energy generation.</p><p>Onset of nuclear <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/gene/">gene</a>-encoded LSS is typically in infancy or early childhood with sudden neurodevelopmental regression. This decompensation is often associated with elevated lactate concentration in the blood and/or cerebrospinal fluid (CSF) following an intercurrent illness or metabolic challenge (e.g., surgery, anesthesia, prolonged fasting). Although the clinical manifestations of LSS can vary, there is significant overlap between the clinical features (see <a href="/sites/books/NBK320989/table/leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn/?report=objectonly" target="object" rid-ob="figobleighnuclovTnuclearencodedleighsyn">Table 1</a>) and biochemical features of both nuclear-encoded LSS and mitochondrial DNA (mtDNA)-encoded LSS.</p><p>Initial clinical features may be nonspecific and may vary with the age of onset. Approximately 75% of individuals present in the first 24 months of life, with some manifesting features in utero.</p><ul><li class="half_rhythm"><div>Prenatally, intrauterine growth restriction, cardiomegaly, microcephaly, and oligohydramnios have been reported.</div></li><li class="half_rhythm"><div>Early-onset LSS (age &#x0003c;2 years) may present with nonspecific features such as poor weight gain, feeding difficulties, persistent vomiting, hypotonia, and developmental delay.</div></li><li class="half_rhythm"><div>Later-onset LSS (age &#x0003e;2 years) may present in childhood, adolescence, or even adulthood with predominant muscle weakness, movement disorders (ataxia and/or dystonia), peripheral neuropathy, and neurobehavioral/psychiatric manifestations.</div></li></ul><p>Other neurologic manifestations include developmental delay/regression, hypotonia, spasticity, seizures, movement disorders (including chorea), cerebellar ataxia, and peripheral neuropathy. Brain stem lesions may cause respiratory issues (apnea, central hypo- or hyperventilation, or irregular respiration), bulbar problems (e.g., abnormal swallowing and speech), and abnormalities of thermoregulation.</p><p>Sensorineural hearing loss is present in approximately 15% of individuals.</p><p>Ophthalmologic manifestations may include ophthalmoplegia, ptosis, optic atrophy, and retinopathy.</p><p>Extraneurologic manifestations can include hypertrophic cardiomyopathy, gastrointestinal dysmotility, hepatopathy, renal tubulopathy, anemia, and hypertrichosis.</p><p>It should be noted that several disorders, e.g., <i>SURF1</i>-related LSS and <i>PDHA1</i>-related LSS, have been associated with a continuous spectrum of disease ranging from LSS to <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/isolated/">isolated</a> features including peripheral neuropathy (Charcot-Marie-Tooth disease) and episodic dystonia.</p><p><b>Typical neuroradiologic findings</b> are bilateral, typically symmetric hyperintensities on T<sub>2</sub>-weighted magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) or hypodensities on computed tomography (CT) in the brain stem and/or basal ganglia, with or without the same findings in the thalamus, cerebellum, subcortical white matter, and/or spinal cord that may fluctuate with time [<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.mccormick.2023.696">McCormick et al 2023</a>] (<a href="https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC10763625/" ref="pagearea=body&amp;targetsite=external&amp;targetcat=link&amp;targettype=uri">full text</a>). Corresponding restricted diffusion may be evident on diffusion-weighted imaging. Additional findings may include cerebral or cerebellar atrophy.</p><p><b>Magnetic resonance spectroscopy (MRS)</b> exhibiting a lactate peak (in the absence of acute seizures) is suggestive of a mitochondrial disorder (including nuclear <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/gene/">gene</a>-encoded LSS).</p><p><b>Prognosis.</b> The prognosis is generally poor. Most affected individuals experience episodic deterioration interspersed with plateaus of stabilization; typically, eventual progressive neurologic decline occurs in stepwise decrements. Up to 50% of individuals die by age three years, most often as a result of respiratory or cardiac failure or neurologic deterioration. However, disease progression can vary and survival into adulthood may occasionally occur.</p><p>Poor prognosis has been associated with early-onset disease and brain stem involvement (e.g., apneas) [<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.sofou.2014.52">Sofou et al 2014</a>, <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.ogawa.2020.819">Ogawa et al 2020</a>, <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.ardissone.2021.413">Ardissone et al 2021</a>].</p><p>Conflicting reports regarding the prognosis of specific genetic causes of LSS can make it difficult to provide adequate counseling for families. <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.ogawa.2020.819">Ogawa et al [2020]</a> reported better outcomes for individuals with <i>ECHS1-</i>, <i>NDUFAF6</i>-, or <i>SURF1</i>-related LSS. While <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.wedatilake.2013.96">Wedatilake et al [2013]</a> also reported better outcomes in <i>SURF1-</i>related LSS, <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.stenton.2022b.466">Stenton et al [2022b]</a> and <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.lim.2022.117">Lim et al [2022]</a> reported poorer prognoses in <i>SURF1</i>-related LSS.</p><p>Survival into adulthood has been reported for individuals with some disorders, including <i>MECR-</i> and <i>MTFMT-</i>related LSS [<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.hayhurst.2019.515">Hayhurst et al 2019</a>, <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.heimer.2016.1229">Heimer et al 2016</a>]<i>.</i></p><div id="leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn" class="table"><h3><span class="label">Table 1. </span></h3><div class="caption"><p>Nuclear-Encoded Leigh Syndrome Spectrum: Frequency of Select Clinical Features</p></div><p class="large-table-link" style="display:none"><span class="right"><a href="/sites/books/NBK320989/table/leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn/?report=objectonly" target="object">View in own window</a></span></p><div class="large_tbl" id="__leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_lrgtbl__"><table class="no_bottom_margin"><thead><tr><th id="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_1" colspan="2" scope="colgroup" rowspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Feature</th><th id="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_2" scope="col" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">% of Persons w/Feature</th><th id="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_3" scope="col" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Comment</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_1" rowspan="12" scope="rowgroup" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<b>Neurologic features</b>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<b>Developmental delay</b>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003e;70%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_1" colspan="1" scope="row" rowspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<b>Hypotonia</b>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">~60%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_1" colspan="1" scope="row" rowspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<b>Developmental regression</b>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">~50%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_1" colspan="1" scope="row" rowspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<b>Spasticity</b>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">~40%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_1" colspan="1" scope="row" rowspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<b>Dystonia</b>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">~35%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_1" colspan="1" scope="row" rowspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<b>Ataxia</b>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">~30%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_1" colspan="1" scope="row" rowspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<b>Muscle weakness</b>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">~30%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_1" colspan="1" scope="row" rowspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<b>Epilepsy</b>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">~30%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_1" colspan="1" scope="row" rowspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<b>Respiratory abnormalities</b>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">~30%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Apnea, hypoventilation, hyperventilation, or irregular respiration</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_1" colspan="1" scope="row" rowspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<b>Dysphagia</b>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">~25%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_1" colspan="1" scope="row" rowspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<b>Sensorineural hearing loss</b>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">~15%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_1" colspan="1" scope="row" rowspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<b>Peripheral neuropathy</b>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">~10%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_1" rowspan="3" scope="rowgroup" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<b>Ophthalmologic features</b>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<b>Ophthalmoplegia</b>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">~25%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_1" colspan="1" scope="row" rowspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<b>Optic atrophy</b>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">~15%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_1" colspan="1" scope="row" rowspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<b>Retinopathy</b>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">~10%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_1" rowspan="6" scope="rowgroup" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<b>Other</b>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<b>Poor weight gain</b>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">~40%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_1" colspan="1" scope="row" rowspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<b>Gastrointestinal manifestations</b>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">~25%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Vomiting, gastroparesis, intestinal dysmotility, constipation</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_1" colspan="1" scope="row" rowspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<b>Cardiac manifestations</b>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">~15%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Hypertrophic or dilated cardiomyopathy; rarely, conduction defects</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_1" colspan="1" scope="row" rowspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<b>Hepatic manifestations</b>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;10%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Elevated liver transaminases, hepatomegaly, or liver failure</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_1" colspan="1" scope="row" rowspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<b>Renal manifestations</b>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;5%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Tubulopathy</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_1" colspan="1" scope="row" rowspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<b>Endocrine manifestations</b>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;5%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.nuclearencoded_leigh_syn_1_1_1_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Diabetes, adrenal insufficiency</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div><div><dl class="temp-labeled-list small"><dt>1. </dt><dd><div id="leigh-nucl-ov.TF.1.1"><p class="no_margin">Estimated percentages are based on several cohorts of individuals with nuclear <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/gene/">gene</a>-encoded LSS and mtDNA-encoded LSS (~1,000 individuals); however, each clinical feature was not reported for every individual [<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.rahman.1996.343">Rahman et al 1996</a>, <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.sofou.2014.52">Sofou et al 2014</a>, <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.ogawa.2017.685">Ogawa et al 2017</a>, <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.sofou.2018.21">Sofou et al 2018</a>, <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.alves.2020.218">Alves et al 2020</a>, <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.hong.2020.267">Hong et al 2020</a>, <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.lee.2020.586">Lee et al 2020</a>, <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.ardissone.2021.413">Ardissone et al 2021</a>, <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.lim.2022.117">Lim et al 2022</a>, <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.stenton.2022b.466">Stenton et al 2022b</a>, <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.tinker.2022.342">Tinker et al 2022</a>, <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.zilber.2023.264">Zilber et al 2023</a>].</p></div></dd></dl></div></div></div><div id="leigh-nucl-ov.Nomenclature"><h3>Nomenclature</h3><p>The term "Leigh syndrome spectrum" comprises both Leigh syndrome and Leigh-like syndrome. "Leigh-like syndrome" has been used historically when clinical and other features strongly suggest Leigh syndrome but do not fulfil the stringent diagnostic criteria for Leigh syndrome because of atypical or normal neuroimaging, lack of evidence of abnormal energy metabolism, atypical neuropathology (variation in the distribution or character of lesions or with the presence of additional unusual features such as extensive cortical destruction), and/or incomplete evaluation. The authors recommend that the term "Leigh-like" no longer be used, as it is now incorporated within the umbrella term LSS.</p></div></div><div id="leigh-nucl-ov.Causes_of_Nuclear_GeneEnco"><h2 id="_leigh-nucl-ov_Causes_of_Nuclear_GeneEnco_">2.  Causes of Nuclear Gene-Encoded Leigh Syndrome Spectrum</h2><p>The pathogenic variants in more than 120 nuclear genes associated with <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/autosomal-recessive/">autosomal recessive</a>, <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/autosomal-dominant/">autosomal dominant</a>, and <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/x-linked/">X-linked</a> nuclear <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/gene/">gene</a>-encoded Leigh syndrome spectrum (LSS) are summarized below [<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.rahman.2017.9">Rahman et al 2017</a>, <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.mccormick.2023.696">McCormick et al 2023</a>] (see Table 2 in <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.mccormick.2023.696">McCormick et al [2023]</a>).</p><p><a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.mccormick.2023.696">McCormick et al [2023]</a> (<a href="https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC10763625/" ref="pagearea=body&amp;targetsite=external&amp;targetcat=link&amp;targettype=uri">full text</a>) determined that the diagnosis of nuclear <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/gene/">gene</a>-encoded LSS is established in a <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/proband/">proband</a> fulfilling clinical criteria for LSS who has one or two pathogenic (or <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/likely-pathogenic/">likely pathogenic</a>) variants in a specific nuclear gene listed in <a href="/sites/books/NBK320989/table/leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig/?report=objectonly" target="object" rid-ob="figobleighnuclovTautosomalrecessiveleig">Tables 2a</a>, <a href="/sites/books/NBK320989/table/leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1/?report=objectonly" target="object" rid-ob="figobleighnuclovTautosomalrecessiveleig1">2b</a>, <a href="/sites/books/NBK320989/table/leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_dominant_leigh/?report=objectonly" target="object" rid-ob="figobleighnuclovTautosomaldominantleigh">3</a>, <a href="/sites/books/NBK320989/table/leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s/?report=objectonly" target="object" rid-ob="figobleighnuclovTxlinkedleighsyndromes">4a</a>, and <a href="/sites/books/NBK320989/table/leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1/?report=objectonly" target="object" rid-ob="figobleighnuclovTxlinkedleighsyndromes1">4b</a> consistent with the expected <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/mode-of-inheritance/">mode of inheritance</a>.</p><div id="leigh-nucl-ov.Clinical_Criteria"><h3>Clinical Criteria</h3><p>Clinical criteria for nuclear <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/gene/">gene</a>-encoded LSS are adapted from Table 2 McCormick et al 2023] (<a href="https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC10763625/" ref="pagearea=body&amp;targetsite=external&amp;targetcat=link&amp;targettype=uri">full text</a>).</p><p><b>Typical neuroradiologic findings.</b> Bilateral, typically symmetric hyperintensities on T<sub>2</sub>-weighted magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) or hypodensities on CT in the brain stem and/or basal ganglia with or without bilateral, T<sub>2</sub>-weighted hyperintensities on MRI or hypodensities on computed tomography (CT) in the thalamus, cerebellum, subcortical white matter, and/or spinal cord.</p><p><b>AND</b> &#x02265;1 of the following <b>characteristic neurologic clinical features</b>:</p><ul><li class="half_rhythm"><div>Developmental regression</div></li><li class="half_rhythm"><div>Developmental delay</div></li><li class="half_rhythm"><div>Neurobehavioral/psychiatric features</div></li></ul><p><b>AND</b> &#x02265;1 of the following <b>biochemical and/or mitochondrial abnormalities</b>:</p><ul><li class="half_rhythm"><div>Elevated concentration of lactate in blood and/or cerebrospinal fluid (CSF)</div></li><li class="half_rhythm"><div>Elevated glycine concentrations (if the responsible <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/gene/">gene</a> is required for biosynthesis of lipoic acid)</div></li><li class="half_rhythm"><div>Magnetic resonance spectroscopy (MRS) lactate peak (in the absence of acute seizures)</div></li><li class="half_rhythm"><div>Respiratory chain enzyme activity deficiency (&#x0003c;30% enzyme activity) in affected tissues (muscle, liver, fibroblasts)</div></li><li class="half_rhythm"><div>Pyruvate dehydrogenase complex deficiency (in fibroblasts, &#x0003e;2 standard deviations below mean)</div></li><li class="half_rhythm"><div>Mitochondrial fission/fusion defect (detected by electron microscopy of muscle or immunofluorescence studies of cultured skin fibroblasts)</div></li><li class="half_rhythm"><div>Diminished respiratory activity measured by microscale oxygraphy (e.g., Oroboros or Seahorse assays)</div></li></ul><p><b>Neuropathologic features of Leigh syndrome.</b> Leigh syndrome was originally defined in 1951 by characteristic neuropathologic features, including multiple focal symmetric necrotic lesions in the basal ganglia, thalamus, brain stem, dentate nuclei, and optic nerves. Histologically, these lesions have a spongiform appearance and are characterized by demyelination, gliosis, and vascular proliferation. Although neuronal loss can occur, typically the neurons are relatively spared.</p><p>The advent of widespread use of MRI made it possible to establish a diagnosis of LSS by neuroimaging, and thus postmortem examination is now rarely performed outside of a research context.</p></div><div id="leigh-nucl-ov.Autosomal_Recessive_Leigh"><h3>Autosomal Recessive Leigh Syndrome Spectrum</h3><p>Autosomal recessive nuclear <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/gene/">gene</a>-encoded LSS (<a href="/sites/books/NBK320989/table/leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig/?report=objectonly" target="object" rid-ob="figobleighnuclovTautosomalrecessiveleig">Table 2a</a> and <a href="/sites/books/NBK320989/table/leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1/?report=objectonly" target="object" rid-ob="figobleighnuclovTautosomalrecessiveleig1">2b</a>) includes:</p><ul><li class="half_rhythm"><div>Pathogenic (or <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/likely-pathogenic/">likely pathogenic</a>) variants in genes encoding proteins needed for:</div><ul><li class="half_rhythm"><div>Oxidative phosphorylation (OXPHOS) enzyme activity and assembly;</div></li><li class="half_rhythm"><div>Mitochondrial DNA (mtDNA) maintenance (see <a href="/sites/books/n/gene/mtdna-md-ov/">Mitochondrial DNA Maintenance Defects Overview</a>) and <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/gene/">gene</a> expression;</div></li><li class="half_rhythm"><div>Cofactor biosynthesis (lipoic acid and coenzyme Q<sub>10</sub> [see <a href="/sites/books/n/gene/coq10-def/">Primary Coenzyme Q<sub>10</sub> Deficiency Overview</a>]);</div></li><li class="half_rhythm"><div>Mitochondrial membrane lipid remodeling, quality control, and dynamics;</div></li><li class="half_rhythm"><div>Pyruvate dehydrogenase (see <a href="/sites/books/n/gene/pdhc-def-ov/">Primary Pyruvate Dehydrogenase Complex Deficiency Overview</a>), biotinidase, and B vitamin transport and metabolism.</div></li></ul></li><li class="half_rhythm"><div>Organic acidemias with accumulation of metabolites leading to secondary OXPHOS dysfunction.</div></li></ul><div id="leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig" class="table"><h3><span class="label">Table 2a. </span></h3><div class="caption"><p>Autosomal Recessive Leigh Syndrome Spectrum with Targeted Therapy: Associated Genes</p></div><p class="large-table-link" style="display:none"><span class="right"><a href="/sites/books/NBK320989/table/leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig/?report=objectonly" target="object">View in own window</a></span></p><div class="large_tbl" id="__leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_lrgtbl__"><table class="no_bottom_margin"><thead><tr><th Features</th><th id="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_4" rowspan="2" scope="col" colspan="1" headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_4" id="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_2_2" scope="col" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Other</th><th headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_3" id="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_2_3" scope="col" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>BTD</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Deafness; optic atrophy; seizures; ataxia</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Alopecia; eczema</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Characteristic organic aciduria; ketolactic acidosis</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of B vitamin transport/metabolism</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a href="/sites/books/n/gene/biotin/">Biotinidase Deficiency</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>COQ4</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Seizures; dystonia; cerebellar atrophy</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Poor growth</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x02193; coenzyme Q<sub>10</sub> (fb), CII&#x000a0;+ III deficiency (fb)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of mt cofactor biosynthesis (coenzyme Q<sub>10</sub>)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"><a href="/sites/books/n/gene/coq10-def/">Primary Coenzyme Q<sub>10</sub> Deficiency Overview</a>, <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.lu.2019.297">Lu et al [2019]</a>, <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.ogawa.2020.819">Ogawa et al [2020]</a></td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>COQ7</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">SNHL; cerebral atrophy</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Oligohydramnios; IUGR; renal dysplasia; HCM</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x02193; coenzyme Q<sub>10</sub> (fb), CII&#x000a0;+ III deficiency (fb)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of mt cofactor biosynthesis (coenzyme Q<sub>10</sub>)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"><a href="/sites/books/n/gene/coq10-def/">Primary Coenzyme Q<sub>10</sub> Deficiency Overview</a>, <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.kwong.2019.23">Kwong et al [2019]</a></td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>COQ9</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">1 person</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Refractory seizures</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Prenatal onset; IUGR; HCM</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complexes I&#x000a0;+ III, II&#x000a0;+ III, &#x00026; coenzyme Q<sub>10</sub> deficiency (m)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of mt cofactor biosynthesis (coenzyme Q<sub>10</sub>)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"><a href="/sites/books/n/gene/coq10-def/">Primary Coenzyme Q<sub>10</sub> Deficiency Overview</a>, <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.smith.2018.719">Smith et al [2018]</a></td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>DLAT</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Episodic dystonia; globus pallidus lesions</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">PDH deficiency (fb)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of pyruvate metabolism</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"><a href="/sites/books/n/gene/pdhc-def-ov/">Primary Pyruvate Dehydrogenase Complex Deficiency Overview</a>, <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.head.2005.234">Head et al [2005]</a></td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>PDHB</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;5%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">CC agenesis/hypoplasia; microcephaly</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">PDH deficiency (fb)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of pyruvate metabolism</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"><a href="/sites/books/n/gene/pdhc-def-ov/">Primary Pyruvate Dehydrogenase Complex Deficiency Overview</a>, <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.quintana.2009.s339">Quintana et al [2009]</a></td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>PDHX</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Thin CC&#x000a0;/ CC agenesis; neonatal lactic acidosis; status epilepticus late in disease (teens/20s)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">PDH deficiency (fb)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of pyruvate metabolism</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"><a href="/sites/books/n/gene/pdhc-def-ov/">Primary Pyruvate Dehydrogenase Complex Deficiency Overview</a>, <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.schiff.2006.709">Schiff et al [2006]</a></td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>PDSS2</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Refractory seizures; SNHL</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Nephrotic syndrome; HCM</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complexes I&#x000a0;+ III, II&#x000a0;+ III, &#x00026; coenzyme Q<sub>10</sub> deficiency (m)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of mt cofactor biosynthesis (coenzyme Q<sub>10</sub>)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a href="/sites/books/n/gene/coq10-def/">Primary Coenzyme Q<sub>10</sub> Deficiency Overview</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>SLC19A3</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;5%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Spasticity; dystonia; seizures; central hypoventilation</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">RCE activity normal</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of B vitamin transport/metabolism</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a href="/sites/books/n/gene/bgd-biotin/">Biotin-Thiamine-Responsive Basal Ganglia Disease</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>SLC25A19</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Bilateral striatal necrosis; episodic encephalopathy w/fever; seizures; chronic progressive polyneuropathy &#x02192; distal weakness &#x00026; contractures</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Enzymology not performed</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of B vitamin transport/metabolism</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">

</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>TPK1</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;5%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Episodic encephalopathy; ataxia; dystonia; seizures; spasticity</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Early onset</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">2-ketoglutaric aciduria</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of B vitamin transport/metabolism</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a href="/sites/books/n/gene/tpk1-def/"><i>TPK1</i>-Related Thiamine Metabolism Dysfunction Syndrome</a>
</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div><div><dl class="temp-labeled-list small"><dt></dt><dd><div><p class="no_margin">AR = <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/autosomal-recessive/">autosomal recessive</a>; CC = corpus callosum; fb = cultured skin fibroblasts; HCM = hypertrophic cardiomyopathy; IUGR = intrauterine growth restriction; LSS = Leigh syndrome spectrum; m = muscle biopsy; MRS = magnetic resonance spectroscopy; MT = mitochondrial; PDH = pyruvate dehydrogenase; RCE = respiratory chain enzyme; SNHL = sensorineural hearing loss</p></div></dd><dt>1. </dt><dd><div id="leigh-nucl-ov.TF.2a.1"><p class="no_margin">Other than those of classic Leigh syndrome</p></div></dd></dl></div></div></div><div id="leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1" class="table"><h3><span class="label">Table 2b. </span></h3><div class="caption"><p>Autosomal Recessive Leigh Syndrome Spectrum: Other Associated Genes</p></div><p class="large-table-link" style="display:none"><span class="right"><a href="/sites/books/NBK320989/table/leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1/?report=objectonly" target="object">View in own window</a></span></p><div class="large_tbl" id="__leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_lrgtbl__"><table class="no_bottom_margin"><thead><tr><th id="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" rowspan="2" scope="rowgroup" colspan="1" headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Gene</th><th id="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="2" scope="col" colspan="1" headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Proportion of Nuclear-Encoded LSS</th><th id="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3" colspan="3" scope="colgroup" rowspan="1" style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle;">Distinguishing Clinical &#x00026; Laboratory Features</th><th id="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="2" scope="col" colspan="1" headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Pathogenic Mechanism</th><th id="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="2" scope="col" colspan="1" headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">References</th></tr><tr><th headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3" id="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" colspan="1" scope="colgroup" rowspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Neurologic&#x000a0;<sup>1</sup></th><th headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3" id="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" scope="col" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Other</th><th headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3" id="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" scope="col" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Laboratory findings</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"><i>ATP5MK</i> (<i>ATP5MD</i>)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Movement disorder; ophthalmoplegia</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">HCM</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x02193; <i>ATP5PO</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Infantile onset; progressive epileptic encephalopathy; progressive cerebral atrophy</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">HCM</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex V deficiency (fb)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex V deficiency</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
