</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>BCS1L</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">SNHL</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Proximal renal tubulopathy; hepatic involvement; pili torti</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex III deficiency (m)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex III deficiency</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.de_lonlay.2001.57">de Lonlay et al [2001]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>CLPB</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Progressive cerebral &#x00026; cerebellar atrophy</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Cataract; neutropenia; HCM</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">3-methylglutaconic aciduria; multiple RCE deficiencies</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of mt protein quality control</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a href="/sites/books/n/gene/clpb-def/">CLPB Deficiency</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>COA6</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">1 person</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">HCM</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex IV deficiency</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex IV deficiency</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.stenton.2022b.466">Stenton et al [2022b]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>COX10</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">SNHL</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">HCM; anemia (due to defect of mt heme A biosynthesis)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex IV deficiency (m)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex IV deficiency</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.antonicka.2003.2693">Antonicka et al [2003]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>COX15</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Seizures, retinopathy</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">HCM, hepatic steatosis</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex IV deficiency (m)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex IV deficiency</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.oquendo.2004.540">Oquendo et al [2004]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>COX4I1</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">1 person</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Microcephaly; seizures; cerebral atrophy</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Short stature</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Multiple RCE deficiencies (m)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex IV deficiency</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.pillai.2019.2138">Pillai et al [2019]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>COX8A</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">1 person</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Seizures; hypotonia; spasticity; leukodystrophy</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex IV deficiency (m)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex IV deficiency</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.hallmann.2016.338">Hallmann et al [2016]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"><i>COXFA4</i> (<i>NDUFA4</i>)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Epilepsy; sensory axonal peripheral neuropathy; leukoencephalopathy</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Slowly progressive; survival into 20s/30s</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex IV deficiency (m)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex IV deficiency</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.pitceathly.2013.1795">Pitceathly et al [2013]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>DLD</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;5%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Episodic encephalopathy</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Hypoglycemia; ketoacidosis; liver failure</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x02191; plasma BCAAs, &#x02191; urine alpha-ketoglutarate, PDH deficiency (fb)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of pyruvate metabolism</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a href="/sites/books/n/gene/dld-def/">Dihydrolipoamide Dehydrogenase Deficiency</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>DNAJC30</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Ataxia; dystonia; ophthalmoparesis; optic atrophy</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex I deficiency (m)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex I deficiency</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.stenton.2022a.1624">Stenton et al [2022a]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"><i>DNM1L</i>&#x000a0;<sup>2</sup></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">1 person</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Hypotonia</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Neonatal onset</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Impaired mt fission (fb)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of mt dynamics</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.hogarth.2018.56">Hogarth et al [2018]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>EARS2</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;5%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Leukoencephalopathy w/thalamus &#x00026; brain stem involvement &#x00026; &#x02191; lactate (MRI); MRI changes may improve w/time.</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Improvement can occur; liver failure in some individuals.</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Multiple RCE deficiencies</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of mt <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/gene/">gene</a> expression</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.martinelli.2012.383">Martinelli et al [2012]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>ECHS1</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">~5%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Psychomotor delay; SNHL; nystagmus; hypotonia; spasticity; athetoid movements; optic atrophy</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">HCM</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x02191; urinary excretion of S-(2-carboxypropyl) cysteine; typically normal RCE w/variable RCE deficiencies in some individuals (m)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">mt toxicity</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a href="/sites/books/n/gene/echs1-def/">Mitochondrial Short-Chain Enoyl-CoA Hydratase 1 Deficiency</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>ETHE1</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Neurodevelopmental delay &#x00026; regression; pyramidal &#x00026; extrapyramidal signs; seizures</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Acrocyanosis; petechiae; frequent vomiting &#x00026; diarrhea in infancy</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Ethylmalonic aciduria</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">mt toxicity</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a href="/sites/books/n/gene/ee/">Ethylmalonic Encephalopathy</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>FARS2</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Severe epilepsy &#x00026; myoclonus; Alpers syndrome neuropathology in some individuals</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Variable RCE deficiencies</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of mt <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/gene/">gene</a> expression</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a href="/sites/books/n/gene/fars2-def/">FARS2 Deficiency</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>FASTKD2</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">1 person</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Seizures; spastic tetraparesis; optic atrophy</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of mt <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/gene/">gene</a> expression</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"><a href="/sites/books/n/gene/fastkd2-coxpd/"><i>FASTKD2</i>-Related Combined Oxidative Phosphorylation Deficiency</a>, <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.gouiza.2024.1259826">Gouiza et al [2024]</a></td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>FBXL4</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;5%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Seizures</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Facial dysmorphism; skeletal abnormalities; poor growth; GI dysmotility; renal tubular acidosis</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Multiple RCE deficiencies</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Dysregulation of mitophagy</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"><a href="/sites/books/n/gene/fbxl4-mtddepl/"><i>FBXL4</i>-Related Encephalomyopathic mtDNA Depletion Syndrome</a>, <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.nguyendien.2023.e112767">Nguyen-Dien et al [2023]</a></td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>FOXRED1</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;5%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Seizures; myoclonus</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Slowly progressive; survival possible into 20s in some</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex I deficiency (m)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex I deficiency</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"><a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.calvo.2010.851">Calvo et al [2010]</a>, <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.fassone.2010.4837">Fassone et al [2010]</a></td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>GFM1</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Axial hypotonia; spasticity; refractory seizures</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Progressive hepatoencephalopathy in some</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Multiple RCE deficiencies</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of mt <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/gene/">gene</a> expression</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.valente.2007.44">Valente et al [2007]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>GFM2</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Multiple RCE deficiencies</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of mt <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/gene/">gene</a> expression</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.fukumura.2015.509">Fukumura et al [2015]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>GTPBP3</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Typically early onset; HCM</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Multiple RCE deficiencies</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of mt <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/gene/">gene</a> expression</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.kopajtich.2014.708">Kopajtich et al [2014]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>HIBCH</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;5%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Developmental regression; seizures; ataxia</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x02191; plasma 4-hydroxybutyrylcarnitine levels; &#x02191; urine 2-methyl-2,3-dihydroxybutyrate; variable deficiency of RCEs &#x00026; PDH</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">mt toxicity</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"><a href="/sites/books/n/gene/hibch-def/">3-Hydroxyisobutyryl-CoA Hydrolase Deficiency</a>, <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.ferdinandusse.2013.188">Ferdinandusse et al [2013]</a></td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>HPDL</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Seizures; spastic paraplegia; microcephaly; visual disturbance</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Early onset</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Variable RCE deficiencies</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of mt cofactor biosynthesis (coenzyme Q<sub>10</sub>)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"><a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.husain.2020.364">Husain et al [2020]</a>, <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.ma.2025.149018">Ma et al [2025]</a></td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>HTRA2</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">1 person</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Nystagmus; hypotonia</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Poor growth</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">CI deficiency (m), no elevation of 3-methylglutaconic acid in this individual</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of mt protein quality control</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.gurusamy.2024.1298574">Gurusamy et al [2024]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>IARS2</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;5%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Infantile spasms w/hypsarrhythmia; diffuse cerebral atrophy; SNHL; peripheral sensory neuropathy</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Cataracts; sideroblastic anemia; growth hormone deficiency; skeletal dysplasia</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x02193; respiratory activity (Oroboros)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of mt <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/gene/">gene</a> expression</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.schwartzentruber.2014.1285">Schwartzentruber et al [2014]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"><i>KGD4</i> (<i>MRPS36</i>)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">2 sibs</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Spastic tetraparesis; hyperkinetic movement disorder; seizures; diffuse cerebral atrophy</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Early onset; HCM</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x02191; plasma glutamine &#x00026; glutamate; low 2-oxoglutarate dehydrogenase complex activity (fb)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">2-oxoglutarate dehydrogenase deficiency</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.galosi.2024.1225">Galosi et al [2024]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>L2HGDH</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">1 person</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x02191; L-2-hydroxyglutaric acid in urine</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">mt toxicity</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.stenton.2022b.466">Stenton et al [2022b]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>LIAS</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Seizures w/burst suppression (EEG)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Mild HCM</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Combined deficiency of PDH&#x000a0;+ glycine cleavage enzyme; &#x02191; urine &#x00026; plasma glycine; deficient lipoylated proteins (western blot)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of mt cofactor biosynthesis (lipoic acid)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.baker.2014.366">Baker et al [2014]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>LIPT1</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Spastic tetraparesis; dystonia</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Liver dysfunction</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x02191; glutamine &#x00026; proline; &#x02193; levels of lysine &#x00026; BCAAs; <i>normal</i> glycine (unlike other lipoic acid synthesis defects); severe &#x02193; of PDH &#x00026; &#x003b1;-KGDH activity; strong &#x02193; of BCKDH activity (fb); normal RCE activity</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of mt cofactor biosynthesis (lipoic acid)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.soreze.2013.192">Soreze et al [2013]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>LONP1</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">1 person</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Neonatal onset</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">mtDNA depletion (m)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of mt protein quality control</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.peter.2018.1743">Peter et al [2018]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>LRPPRC</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;5%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Metabolic (acidosis &#x00026; ketosis) &#x00026; neurologic (stroke-like) crises; seizures</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Survival 5 days to 30 yrs; median age at death 1.6 yrs</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex IV deficiency (m)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex IV deficiency</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"><a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.mootha.2003.605">Mootha et al [2003]</a>, <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.debray.2011.183">Debray et al [2011]</a></td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>MECR</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Dystonia; chorea; optic atrophy</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Survival to adulthood</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Reduced protein lipoylation; &#x02193; respiratory activity (fb)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of mt cofactor biosynthesis (lipoic acid)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"><a href="/sites/books/n/gene/mecr-dis/"><i>MECR</i>-Related Neurologic Disorder,</a>
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.heimer.2016.1229">Heimer et al [2016]</a></td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>MFF</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Seizures; optic atrophy; peripheral neuropathy; microcephaly; cerebellar atrophy</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Multiple RCE deficiencies; elongated mitochondria &#x00026; peroxisomes (EM)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of mt dynamics</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.koch.2016.270">Koch et al [2016]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>MFN2</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">1 person</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Seizures; psychiatric disturbance; optic atrophy; dystonia; chorea; sensorimotor neuropathy</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Hypothyroidism</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Multiple mtDNA deletions (m)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of mt dynamics</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.souza.2019.25">Souza et al [2019]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>MPC1</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">1 person</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Microcephaly; seizures</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Poor growth</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of pyruvate metabolism</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.jiang.2022.264">Jiang et al [2022]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>MPV17</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Peripheral neuropathy; ataxia; SNHL</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Episodic hypoglycemia &#x00026; metabolic acidosis; prolonged jaundice</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">CI &#x00026; CIII deficient activity; mtDNA depletion (m)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">mtDNA maintenance defect</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"><a href="/sites/books/n/gene/mpv17-mtdep/"><i>MPV17</i>-Related mtDNA Maintenance Defect</a>, <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.souza.2019.25">Souza et al [2019]</a></td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>MRPL3</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">SNHL</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Early onset; cataracts; liver dysfunction; HCM</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Multiple RCE deficiencies (m)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of mt <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/gene/">gene</a> expression</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.alsharhan.2021.2519">Alsharhan et al [2021]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>MRPL39</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">2 persons</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Seizures</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Early onset; HCM</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of mt <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/gene/">gene</a> expression</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.amarasekera.2023.2441">Amarasekera et al [2023]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>MRPS34</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Microcephaly</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Typically early onset, but survival to adulthood has been reported</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Multiple RCE deficiencies</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of mt <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/gene/">gene</a> expression</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.lake.2017.239">Lake et al [2017]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>MTFMT</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;5%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Cystic leukoencephalopathy in some; typically shows a milder clinical course</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">May be slowly progressive in some, w/survival into 20s</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Multiple RCE deficiencies</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of mt <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/gene/">gene</a> expression</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"><a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.tucker.2011.428">Tucker et al [2011]</a>, <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.hayhurst.2019.515">Hayhurst et al [2019]</a></td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"><i>MTRFR</i> (<i>C12orf65</i>)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Ophthalmoplegia; optic atrophy; axonal neuropathy</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Relatively slow disease progression</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Multiple RCE deficiencies (fb)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of mt <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/gene/">gene</a> expression</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.antonicka.2010.115">Antonicka et al [2010]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>MTO1</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">HCM</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of mt <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/gene/">gene</a> expression</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.stenton.2022b.466">Stenton et al [2022b]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"><i>NARS2</i>&#x000a0;<sup>3</sup></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">SNHL</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Multiple RCE deficiencies</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of mt <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/gene/">gene</a> expression</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.simon.2015.e1005097">Simon et al [2015]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>NAXE</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Encephalopathy assoc w/febrile illness; cerebellar atrophy</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Erythematous rash (1 person); nephrotic syndrome (1 person)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Normal RCE in 1 person (m)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">mt toxicity</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.kremer.2016.894">Kremer et al [2016]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>NDUFA10</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">HCM</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex I deficiency (m)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex I deficiency</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.hoefs.2011.270">Hoefs et al [2011]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>NDUFA12</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Severe dystonia</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Hypertrichosis</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex I deficiency (m)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex I deficiency</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.ostergaard.2011.737">Ostergaard et al [2011]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>NDUFA13</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex I deficiency (m)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex I deficiency</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.angebault.2015.3948">Angebault et al [2015]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>NDUFA2</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Cystic Leukoencephalopathy</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">HCM</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex I deficiency (m)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex I deficiency</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
