</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>NDUFS4</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;5%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Early onset; HCM</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex I deficiency (m)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex I deficiency</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.budde.2000.63">Budde et al [2000]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>NDUFS6</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Episodic deterioration w/hypoventilation</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Early onset</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Multiple RCE deficiencies (m); BN-PAGE assembly defect of complex I (fb)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex I deficiency</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.rouzier.2019.637">Rouzier et al [2019]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>NDUFS7</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex I deficiency (m)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex I deficiency</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.triepels.1999.787">Triepels et al [1999]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>NDUFS8</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Leukodystrophy</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">HCM</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex I deficiency (m)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex I deficiency</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.loeffen.1998.1598">Loeffen et al [1998]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>NDUFV1</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">~5%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Cystic leukoencephalopathy; dystonia</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex I deficiency (m)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex I deficiency</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.b_nit.2001.1344">B&#x000e9;nit et al [2001]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>NDUFV2</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Spasticity</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Optic atrophy; HCM</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex I deficiency (m)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex I deficiency</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.cameron.2015.525">Cameron et al [2015]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>NUBPL</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Characteristic MRI changes: predominant abnormalities of cerebellar cortex, deep cerebral white matter, &#x00026; corpus callosum</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex I deficiency (m)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex I deficiency</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.calvo.2010.851">Calvo et al [2010]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"><i>OPA1</i>&#x000a0;<sup>4</sup></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Ataxia; seizures; peripheral neuropathy; spasticity; optic atrophy; cerebellar atrophy</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Variable RCE results (m)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of mt dynamics</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"><a href="/sites/books/n/gene/mtdna-md-ov/">Mitochondrial DNA Maintenance Defects Overview</a>, <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.nasca.2017.89">Nasca et al [2017]</a></td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>PET100</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;5%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Prominent myoclonus &#x00026; seizures; spastic tetraparesis</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Survival to 20s (50%)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex IV deficiency (m)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex IV deficiency</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.lim.2014.209">Lim et al [2014]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>PET117</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">1 family</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex IV deficiency (m)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex IV deficiency</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.renkema.2017.759">Renkema et al [2017]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>PMPCA</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Spastic paraplegia; ataxia; cerebellar atrophy</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Fragmented mitochondria (fb) in 1 person</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect in mt protein quality control</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.rubegni.2019.e168">Rubegni et al [2019]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>PMPCB</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Ataxia; seizures; cerebellar atrophy</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Multiple RCE deficiencies; &#x02193; aconitase activity (m) in 1 person</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect in mt protein quality control</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"><a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.v_gtle.2018.557">V&#x000f6;gtle et al [2018]</a>, <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.matthews.2024.283">Matthews et al [2024]</a></td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>PNPT1</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Choreoathetosis &#x00026; dyskinesia; also <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/isolated/">isolated</a> SNHL</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Severe hypotonia</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex III&#x000a0;+ IV deficiency in liver in 1 person (normal activity in m &#x00026; fb)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of mt <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/gene/">gene</a> expression</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.vedrenne.2012.912">Vedrenne et al [2012]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>POLG</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Roving eye movements; prominent seizures; more often presents as Alpers or other epilepsy syndromes than LSS</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Hepatocerebral disease</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Multiple RCE deficiencies; <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/isolated/">isolated</a> complex IV deficiency (rare)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">mtDNA maintenance defect</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"><a href="/sites/books/n/gene/alpers/"><i>POLG</i>-Related Disorders</a>, <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.taanman.2009.248">Taanman et al [2009]</a></td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>PTCD3</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Seizures; optic atrophy; SNHL</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Multiple RCE deficiencies</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of mt <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/gene/">gene</a> expression</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.borna.2019.9">Borna et al [2019]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>RNASEH1</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">1 person</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Cerebellar ataxia; psychosis; ophthalmoplegia; polyneuropathy</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Late onset</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Multiple mtDNA deletions, complex I deficiency (m)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">mtDNA maintenance defect</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"><a href="/sites/books/n/gene/mtdna-md-ov/">Mitochondrial DNA Maintenance Defects Overview</a>, <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.souza.2019.25">Souza et al [2019]</a></td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>RRM2B</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Ophthalmoplegia; ataxia; SNHL; demyelinating polyneuropathy</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">HCM</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">mtDNA depletion (m)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">mtDNA maintenance defect</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"><a href="/sites/books/n/gene/rrm2b-mtddepl/"><i>RRM2B</i> Mitochondrial DNA Maintenance Defects</a>, <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.souza.2019.25">Souza et al [2019]</a></td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>SCO2</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;5%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Early onset; severe muscle weakness</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">HCM; febrile syndrome not assoc w/infections</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex IV deficiency (m)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex IV deficiency</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"><a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.joost.2010.227">Joost et al [2010]</a>, <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.kistol.2023.24">Kistol et al [2023]</a></td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>SDHA</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Course may be indolent w/survival into adulthood in some; HCM</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex II deficiency (m); succinate peak (brain MRS)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex II deficiency</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"><a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.bourgeron.1995.144">Bourgeron et al [1995]</a>, <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.pagnamenta.2006.214">Pagnamenta et al [2006]</a></td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>SDHAF1</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;5%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Leukoencephalopathy on MRI (1 person w/neuropathologic LS)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex II deficiency (m); succinate peak (brain MRS)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex II deficiency</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.ohlenbusch.2012.69">Ohlenbusch et al [2012]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>SDHB</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Optic atrophy (1 person)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex II deficiency</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.kaur.2020.345">Kaur et al [2020]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>SERAC1</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;5%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">SNHL; distinct brain MRI pattern</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">MEG(H)DEL syndrome; may have liver involvement in infancy that later normalizes</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">3-methylglutaconic aciduria; variable RCE deficiencies</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of mt membrane lipids</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a href="/sites/books/n/gene/megdel/">SERAC1 Deficiency</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>SLC25A46</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">2 persons</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Seizures; spastic diplegia; optic atrophy; pontocerebellar hypoplasia</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x02191; mt connectivity</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of mt dynamics</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"><a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.abrams.2015.926">Abrams et al [2015]</a>, <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.janer.2016.1019">Janer et al [2016]</a></td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>SPG7</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">1 person</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Spastic paraparesis; ophthalmoplegia; optic atrophy; psychosis; abnormal movements; global cerebral atrophy</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Multiple mtDNA deletions (m)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of mt protein quality control</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.souza.2019.25">Souza et al [2019]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>SQOR</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">2 families</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Episodic encephalopathy following infections</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Liver failure in 1 person</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex IV deficiency in 1 person (m &#x00026; liver); RCE activity in fb normal in 1 person</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">mt toxicity</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.friederich.2020.1024">Friederich et al [2020]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>SUCLA2</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;5%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Hypotonia; muscle atrophy; hyperkinesia; severe SNHL</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Poor growth</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x02191; urine &#x00026; plasma MMA; multiple RCE deficiencies</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">mt DNA maintenance defect</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a href="/sites/books/n/gene/sucla2-def/"><i>SUCLA2</i>-Related mtDNA Depletion Syndrome, Encephalomyopathic Form with Methylmalonic Aciduria</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>SUCLG1</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Severe myopathy; SNHL; dystonia</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Recurrent hepatic failure; poor growth; HCM</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x02191; urine &#x00026; plasma MMA; multiple RCE deficiencies</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">mt DNA maintenance defect</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a href="/sites/books/n/gene/suclg1-mtddepl/"><i>SUCLG1</i>-Related mtDNA Depletion Syndrome, Encephalomyopathic Form with Methylmalonic Aciduria</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>SURF1</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">~50% of complex IV-deficient LS (~30% of nuclear-encoded LS)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Developmental regression (71%); nystagmus&#x000a0;+ ophthalmoplegia (52%); movement disorder (52%); peripheral neuropathy</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Typically early onset; hypertrichosis (48%); median survival 5.4 yrs</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex IV deficiency (more severe fb than m)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex IV deficiency</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.wedatilake.2013.96">Wedatilake et al [2013]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>TACO1</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;5%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Cognitive dysfunction; dystonia; visual impairment; periventricular white matter lesions</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Late onset (age 4-16 yrs); slowly progressive</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex IV deficiency (m)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex IV deficiency</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"><a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.weraarpachai.2009.833">Weraarpachai et al [2009]</a>, <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.oktay.2020.301">Oktay et al [2020]</a></td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>TARS2</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;5%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Microcephaly; seizure; cerebellar atrophy; CC dysplasia</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Early onset</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Multiple RCE deficiencies</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of mt <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/gene/">gene</a> expression</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.diodato.2014.983">Diodato et al [2014]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>TIMMDC1</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">1 person</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Cerebellar syndrome; basal ganglia abnormalities (CT); subsequent MRI unremarkable</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex I deficiency (m)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex I deficiency</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.kremer.2017.15824">Kremer et al [2017]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>TMEM126B</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">1 person</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Gait disturbance</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Liver dysfunction</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex I deficiency</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.zhou.2023.239">Zhou et al [2023]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>TOMM7</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">1 person</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Early onset; hypotonia; dystonia; optic atrophy</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of mt membranes</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.yeole.2025.e63892">Yeole et al [2025]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>TRMU</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Usually causes benign reversible liver failure w/o neurologic symptoms</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Multiple RCE deficiencies</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of mt <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/gene/">gene</a> expression</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a href="/sites/books/n/gene/trmu-def/">TRMU Deficiency</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>TSFM</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Juvenile onset; ataxia; hyperkinetic movement disorder; neuropathy; optic atrophy</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Poor growth; HCM</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Multiple RCE deficiencies</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of mt <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/gene/">gene</a> expression</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.ahola.2014.743">Ahola et al [2014]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>TTC19</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;5%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Ataxia; severe olivopontocerebellar atrophy</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Slowly progressive; survival into 20s/30s</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex III deficiency (m)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex III deficiency</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.ghezzi.2011.259">Ghezzi et al [2011]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>TWNK</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">1 person</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Ptosis; ophthalmoplegia; optic atrophy; SNHL; sensory axonal neuropathy</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Cataracts</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">mtDNA depletion, multiple mtDNA deletions (m)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">mtDNA maintenance defect</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"><a href="/sites/books/n/gene/mtdna-md-ov/">Mitochondrial DNA Maintenance Defects Overview</a>, <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.souza.2019.25">Souza et al [2019]</a></td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>UQCRC2</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Reye syndrome w/metabolic acidosis &#x00026; hypoglycemia</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex III deficiency (fb)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex III deficiency</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"><a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.burska.2021.166147">Burska et al [2021]</a>, <a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.bansept.2023.138">Bansept et al [2023]</a></td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>UQCRQ</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">1 family</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Dystonia; ataxia</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Slowly progressive; survival into 30s</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex III deficiency (m)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex III deficiency</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.barel.2008.1211">Barel et al [2008]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>VPS13D</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Ataxia; seizures</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"></td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Dysregulation of mitophagy</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.gauthier.2018.1089">Gauthier et al [2018]</a>
</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div><div><dl class="temp-labeled-list small"><dt></dt><dd><div><p class="no_margin">&#x003b1;-KGDH = alpha-ketoglutarate dehydrogenase; BCAA = branched-chain amino acid; BCKDH = branched-chain ketoacid dehydrogenase; BN-PAGE = blue native polyacrylamide gel electrophoresis; CC = corpus callosum; CT = computed tomography; EEG = electroencephalogram; EM = electron microscopy; fb = cultured skin fibroblasts; FILA = fatal infantile lactic acidosis; GI = gastrointestinal; HCM = hypertrophic cardiomyopathy; IUGR = intrauterine growth restriction; LS = Leigh syndrome; LSS = Leigh syndrome spectrum; m = muscle biopsy; MEGD(H)EL syndrome = 3-<i>me</i>thyl<i>g</i>lutaconic aciduria with <i>d</i>eafness-dystonia, (<i>h</i>epatopathy), <i>e</i>ncephalopathy, and Leigh-like syndrome; MMA = methylmalonic aciduria; MRS = magnetic resonance spectroscopy; mt = mitochondrial; PDH = pyruvate dehydrogenase; RCE = respiratory chain enzyme; SNHL = sensorineural hearing loss</p></div></dd><dt>1. </dt><dd><div id="leigh-nucl-ov.TF.2b.1"><p class="no_margin">Other than those of classic Leigh syndrome</p></div></dd><dt>2. </dt><dd><div id="leigh-nucl-ov.TF.2b.2"><p class="no_margin">Autosomal dominant <i>DNM1L</i>-related LSS has also been reported (see <a href="/sites/books/NBK320989/table/leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_dominant_leigh/?report=objectonly" target="object" rid-ob="figobleighnuclovTautosomaldominantleigh">Table 3</a>).</p></div></dd><dt>3. </dt><dd><div id="leigh-nucl-ov.TF.2b.3"><p class="no_margin">Isolated SNHL without Leigh syndrome in some individuals; Alpers syndrome in others</p></div></dd><dt>4. </dt><dd><div id="leigh-nucl-ov.TF.2b.4"><p class="no_margin">Autosomal dominant <i>OPA1</i>-related LSS has also been reported (see <a href="/sites/books/NBK320989/table/leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_dominant_leigh/?report=objectonly" target="object" rid-ob="figobleighnuclovTautosomaldominantleigh">Table 3</a>).</p></div></dd></dl></div></div></div></div><div id="leigh-nucl-ov.Autosomal_Dominant_Leigh_S"><h3>Autosomal Dominant Leigh Syndrome Spectrum</h3><p>Causes of <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/autosomal-dominant/">autosomal dominant</a> nuclear <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/gene/">gene</a>-encoded LSS are summarized in <a href="/sites/books/NBK320989/table/leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_dominant_leigh/?report=objectonly" target="object" rid-ob="figobleighnuclovTautosomaldominantleigh">Table 3</a>. Two genes, <i>DNM1L</i> and <i>OPA1</i>, have been associated with both autosomal dominant LSS and <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/autosomal-recessive/">autosomal recessive</a> LSS and, thus, have been included in both <a href="/sites/books/NBK320989/table/leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_recessive_leig_1/?report=objectonly" target="object" rid-ob="figobleighnuclovTautosomalrecessiveleig1">Table 2b</a> and <a href="/sites/books/NBK320989/table/leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_dominant_leigh/?report=objectonly" target="object" rid-ob="figobleighnuclovTautosomaldominantleigh">Table 3</a>. No targeted treatments are available for autosomal dominant nuclear gene-encoded LSS.</p><div id="leigh-nucl-ov.T.autosomal_dominant_leigh" class="table"><h3><span class="label">Table 3. </span></h3><div class="caption"><p>Autosomal Dominant Leigh Syndrome Spectrum: Associated Genes</p></div><p class="large-table-link" style="display:none"><span class="right"><a Features</th><th id="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_4" rowspan="2" scope="col" colspan="1" headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_4" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Pathologic Mechanism</th><th id="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_5" rowspan="2" scope="col" colspan="1" headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_5" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Reference</th></tr><tr><th headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_3" id="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_2_1" colspan="1" scope="colgroup" rowspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Neurologic&#x000a0;<sup>1</sup></th><th headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_3" id="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_2_2" scope="col" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Other</th><th headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_3" id="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_2_3" scope="col" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Laboratory findings</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>PDHA1</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">~10%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Developmental delay; hypotonia; seizures; choreoathetosis; dystonia; episodic ataxia in some; microcephaly; cerebral atrophy; cystic lesions in basal ganglia, brain stem, &#x00026; cerebral hemispheres; agenesis of CC</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Facial dysmorphism</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_2_3" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x02193;/&#x02193;-normal lactate-to-pyruvate ratio in blood &#x00026; CSF; PDH deficiency (fb)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of pyruvate metabolism</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a href="/sites/books/n/gene/pdhc-def-ov/">Primary Pyruvate Dehydrogenase Complex Deficiency Overview</a>
</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div><div><dl class="temp-labeled-list small"><dt></dt><dd><div><p class="no_margin">CC = corpus callosum; CSF = cerebrospinal fluid; fb = cultured skin fibroblasts; LSS = Leigh syndrome spectrum; PDH = pyruvate dehydrogenase</p></div></dd><dt>1. </dt><dd><div id="leigh-nucl-ov.TF.4a.1"><p class="no_margin">Other than those of classic Leigh syndrome</p></div></dd></dl></div></div></div><div id="leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1" class="table"><h3><span class="label">Table 4b. </span></h3><div class="caption"><p>X-Linked Leigh Syndrome Spectrum: Other Genes</p></div><p class="large-table-link" style="display:none"><span class="right"><a href="/sites/books/NBK320989/table/leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1/?report=objectonly" target="object">View in own window</a></span></p><div class="large_tbl" id="__leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_lrgtbl__"><table class="no_bottom_margin"><thead><tr><th id="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_1_1" rowspan="2" scope="rowgroup" colspan="1" headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_1_1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Gene</th><th id="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="2" scope="col" colspan="1" headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_1_2" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Proportion of Nuclear-Encoded LSS</th><th id="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_1_3" colspan="2" scope="colgroup" rowspan="1" style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle;">Distinguishing Clinical &#x00026; Laboratory Features</th><th id="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="2" scope="col" colspan="1" headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_1_4" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Pathologic Mechanism</th><th id="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="2" scope="col" colspan="1" headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_1_5" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Reference</th></tr><tr><th headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_1_3" id="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_2_1" colspan="1" scope="colgroup" rowspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Neurologic&#x000a0;<sup>1</sup></th><th headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_1_3" id="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_2_2" scope="col" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Laboratory findings</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>AIFM1</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Encephalomyopathy w/bilateral striatal lesions</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Multiple RCE deficiencies (m)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Dysregulation of mitophagy</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.ghezzi.2010.639">Ghezzi et al [2010]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>HSD17B10</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Seizures; ataxia; microcephaly; visual impairment; movement disorders; diffuse cerebral atrophy</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x02191; urine 2-methyl-3-hydroxybutyrate &#x00026; tiglylglycine</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of mitochondrial <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/gene/">gene</a> expression</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.upadia.2021.35">Upadia et al [2021]</a>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>NDUFA1</i>
</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">&#x0003c;1%</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_2_1" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">DD; axial hypotonia; nystagmus; choreoathetosis; myoclonic epilepsy; survival to 30s in 2 persons; spinal cord lesions in 1 person</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_1_3 hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_2_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex I deficiency (m)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_1_4" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex I deficiency</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.xlinked_leigh_syndrome_s_1_1_1_1_5" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<a class="bk_pop" href="#leigh-nucl-ov.REF.fernandezmoreira.2007.73">Fernandez-Moreira et al [2007]</a>
</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div><div><dl class="temp-labeled-list small"><dt></dt><dd><div><p class="no_margin">DD = developmental delay; LSS = Leigh syndrome spectrum; m = muscle biopsy; RCE = respiratory chain enzyme</p></div></dd><dt>1. </dt><dd><div id="leigh-nucl-ov.TF.4b.1"><p class="no_margin">Other than those of classic Leigh syndrome</p></div></dd></dl></div></div></div></div><div id="leigh-nucl-ov.Pathologic_Mechanism_of_Ge"><h3>Pathologic Mechanism of Genes in Nuclear-Encoded Leigh Syndrome Spectrum</h3><div id="leigh-nucl-ov.T.pathologic_mechanism_of" class="table"><h3><span class="label">Table 5. </span></h3><div class="caption"><p>Pathologic Mechanism of Genes in Nuclear-Encoded LSS</p></div><p class="large-table-link" style="display:none"><span class="right"><a href="/sites/books/NBK320989/table/leigh-nucl-ov.T.pathologic_mechanism_of/?report=objectonly" target="object">View in own window</a></span></p><div class="large_tbl" id="__leigh-nucl-ov.T.pathologic_mechanism_of_lrgtbl__"><table class="no_bottom_margin"><thead><tr><th id="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.pathologic_mechanism_of_1_1_1_1" scope="col" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Pathologic Mechanism</th><th id="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.pathologic_mechanism_of_1_1_1_2" scope="col" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Genes</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.pathologic_mechanism_of_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex I deficiency</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.pathologic_mechanism_of_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>DNAJC30, FOXRED1, NDUFA1, NDUFA2, NDUFA9, NDUFA10, NDUFA12, NDUFA13, NDUFAF2, NDUFAF3, NDUFAF4, NDUFAF5, NDUFAF6, NDUFAF8, NDUFB8, NDUFC2, NDUFS1, NDUFS2, NDUFS3, NDUFS4, NDUFS6, NDUFS7, NDUFS8, NDUFV1, NDUFV2, NUBPL, TIMMDC1, TMEM126B</i>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.pathologic_mechanism_of_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex II deficiency</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.pathologic_mechanism_of_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>SDHA, SDHAF1, SDHB</i>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.pathologic_mechanism_of_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex III deficiency</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.pathologic_mechanism_of_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>BCS1L, TTC19, UQCRC2, UQCRQ</i>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.pathologic_mechanism_of_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex IV deficiency</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.pathologic_mechanism_of_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"><i>COA6, COX4I1, COX8A, COX10, COX15, COXFA4</i> (<i>NDUFA4</i>), LRPPRC<i>, PET100, PET117, SCO2, SURF1, TACO1</i></td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.pathologic_mechanism_of_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Complex V deficiency</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.pathologic_mechanism_of_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"><i>ATP5PO, ATP5MK</i> (<i>ATP5MD</i>)</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.pathologic_mechanism_of_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of pyruvate metabolism</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.pathologic_mechanism_of_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>DLAT, DLD, MPC1, PDHA1, PDHB, PDHX</i>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.pathologic_mechanism_of_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of B vitamin transport/metabolism</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.pathologic_mechanism_of_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>BTD, SLC19A3, SLC25A19, TPK1</i>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.pathologic_mechanism_of_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of cofactor biosynthesis (coenzyme Q<sub>10</sub>)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.pathologic_mechanism_of_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>COQ4, COQ7, COQ9, HPDL, PDSS2</i>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.pathologic_mechanism_of_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of cofactor biosynthesis (lipoic acid)</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.pathologic_mechanism_of_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>LIAS, LIPT1, MECR</i>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.pathologic_mechanism_of_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of mtDNA maintenance</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.pathologic_mechanism_of_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>MPV17, POLG, RNASEH1, RRM2B, SLC25A4, SSBP1, SUCLA2, SUCLG, TWNK</i>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.pathologic_mechanism_of_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of mitochondrial <a class="def" href="/sites/books/n/gene/glossary/def-item/gene/">gene</a> expression</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.pathologic_mechanism_of_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;"><i>EARS2, FARS2, FASTKD2, GFM1, GFM2, GTPBP3, HSD17B10, IARS2, MRPL3, MRPL39, MRPS34, MTFMT, MTO1, MTRFR</i> (<i>C12ORF65</i>)<i>, NARS2, PNPT1, PTCD3, TARS2, TRMU, TSFM</i></td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.pathologic_mechanism_of_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of mitochondrial protein quality control</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.pathologic_mechanism_of_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>CLPB, HRTA2, LONP1, PMPCA, PMPCB, SPG7</i>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.pathologic_mechanism_of_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of mitochondrial membranes</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.pathologic_mechanism_of_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>SERAC1, TOMM7</i>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.pathologic_mechanism_of_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Defect of mitochondrial dynamics</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.pathologic_mechanism_of_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>DNM1L, MFF, MFN2, OPA1, SLC25A46</i>
</td></tr><tr><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.pathologic_mechanism_of_1_1_1_1" scope="row" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">Dysregulation of mitophagy</td><td headers="hd_h_leigh-nucl-ov.T.pathologic_mechanism_of_1_1_1_2" rowspan="1" colspan="1" style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle;">
<i>AIFM1, FBXL4, VPS13D</i>
